@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.HasRiemannAccuracy : {m : Nat} →
  {law : NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Law m} →
    {Result : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem m law → Type u_1} →
      {Information : Type u_2} →
        @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine m law Result Information →
          (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem m law → Real) → Prop
def NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.HasRiemannAccuracy.{u_1, u_2} : {m : Nat} →
  {law : NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Law m} →
    {Result : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem m law → Type u_1} →
      {Information : Type u_2} →
        @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine m law Result Information →
          (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem m law → Real) → Prop :=
fun {m} {law} {Result} {Information} routine errorBound =>
  ∀ (problem : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem m law)
    (admitted : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.domain m law Result Information routine problem),
    ∃ reference,
      @LE.le Real Real.instLE
        (@Norm.norm (Fin m → Real)
          (@NormedRing.toNorm (Fin m → Real)
            (@Pi.normedRing (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m) fun i =>
              @NormedCommRing.toNormedRing Real Real.normedCommRing))
          (@HSub.hSub (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real)
            (@instHSub (Fin m → Real) (@Pi.instSub (Fin m) (fun a => Real) fun i => Real.instSub))
            (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.flux m law Result Information routine problem admitted)
            (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Reference.meanFlux m law problem reference)))
        (errorBound problem)
'NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.HasRiemannAccuracy' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Method.toRoutine_hasRiemannAccuracy : ∀ {m : Nat}
  {law : NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Law m}
  {Result : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem m law → Type u_1} {Information : Type u_2}

  @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.HasRiemannAccuracy m law Result Information
    (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Method.toRoutine m law Result Information method)
    (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Method.errorBound m law Result Information method)

@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.HasRiemannAccuracy.reference_comparison : ∀ {m : Nat}
  {law : NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Law m}
  {Result : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem m law → Type u_1} {Information : Type u_2}
  {routine : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine m law Result Information}
  {errorBound : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem m law → Real},
  @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.HasRiemannAccuracy m law Result Information routine errorBound →
    ∀ (problem : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem m law)
      (admitted : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.domain m law Result Information routine problem),
      ∃ reference,
        And
          (@NumStability.IsRiemannData (Fin m → Real)
            (fun x =>
              @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Reference.field m law problem reference x
                (@OfNat.ofNat Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0 Real Real.instZero)))
            (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem.left m law problem)
            (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem.right m law problem))
          (And
            (@NumStability.IsOneDimensionalCellAverage (Fin m → Real)
              (@Pi.normedAddCommGroup (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m) fun i => Real.normedAddCommGroup)
              (@Pi.normedSpace Real Real.normedField (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m)
                (fun i =>
                  @NonUnitalSeminormedRing.toSeminormedAddCommGroup Real
                    (@NonUnitalSeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedRing Real
                      (@SeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedCommRing Real
                        (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing Real Real.normedCommRing))))
                fun i =>
                @InnerProductSpace.toNormedSpace Real Real Real.instRCLike
                  (@NonUnitalSeminormedRing.toSeminormedAddCommGroup Real
                    (@NonUnitalSeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedRing Real
                      (@SeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedCommRing Real
                        (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing Real Real.normedCommRing))))
                  (@RCLike.toInnerProductSpaceReal Real Real.instRCLike))
              (fun τ =>
                @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Law.flux m law
                  (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Reference.field m law problem reference
                    (@OfNat.ofNat Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0 Real Real.instZero)) τ))
              (@OfNat.ofNat Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0 Real Real.instZero))
              (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem.duration m law problem)
              (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Reference.meanFlux m law problem reference))
            (And
              (@LE.le Real Real.instLE (@OfNat.ofNat Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0 Real Real.instZero))
                (errorBound problem))
              (And
                (@LE.le Real Real.instLE
                  (@Norm.norm (Fin m → Real)
                    (@NormedRing.toNorm (Fin m → Real)
                      (@Pi.normedRing (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m) fun i =>
                        @NormedCommRing.toNormedRing Real Real.normedCommRing))
                    (@HSub.hSub (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real)
                      (@instHSub (Fin m → Real) (@Pi.instSub (Fin m) (fun a => Real) fun i => Real.instSub))
                      (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.flux m law Result Information routine problem
                        admitted)
                      (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Reference.meanFlux m law problem reference)))
                  (errorBound problem))
                (∀ (physicalMean : Fin m → Real) (comparisonBound : Real),
                  @LE.le Real Real.instLE
                      (@Norm.norm (Fin m → Real)
                        (@NormedRing.toNorm (Fin m → Real)
                          (@Pi.normedRing (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m) fun i =>
                            @NormedCommRing.toNormedRing Real Real.normedCommRing))
                        (@HSub.hSub (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real)
                          (@instHSub (Fin m → Real) (@Pi.instSub (Fin m) (fun a => Real) fun i => Real.instSub))
                          (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Reference.meanFlux m law problem reference)
                          physicalMean))
                      comparisonBound →
                    @LE.le Real Real.instLE
                      (@Norm.norm (Fin m → Real)
                        (@NormedRing.toNorm (Fin m → Real)
                          (@Pi.normedRing (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m) fun i =>
                            @NormedCommRing.toNormedRing Real Real.normedCommRing))
                        (@HSub.hSub (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real)
                          (@instHSub (Fin m → Real) (@Pi.instSub (Fin m) (fun a => Real) fun i => Real.instSub))
                          (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.flux m law Result Information routine problem
                            admitted)
                          physicalMean))
                      (@HAdd.hAdd Real Real Real (@instHAdd Real Real.instAdd) (errorBound problem) comparisonBound)))))

@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.certifiedRoutine_local_interface_contract : ∀ {m : Nat}

  (routine : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine m law Result Information)
  (errorBound : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem m law → Real),
  @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.HasRiemannAccuracy m law Result Information routine errorBound →
    ∀ (left center right : Fin m → Real)
      (hleftState :
        @Membership.mem (Fin m → Real) (Set (Fin m → Real)) (@Set.instMembership (Fin m → Real))
          (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Law.states m law) left)
      (hcenterState :
        @Membership.mem (Fin m → Real) (Set (Fin m → Real)) (@Set.instMembership (Fin m → Real))
          (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Law.states m law) center)
      (hrightState :
        @Membership.mem (Fin m → Real) (Set (Fin m → Real)) (@Set.instMembership (Fin m → Real))
          (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Law.states m law) right)
      {a b s t : Real},
      @LT.lt Real Real.instLT a b →
        ∀ (hst : @LT.lt Real Real.instLT s t)
          (hleftDomain :
            @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.domain m law Result Information routine
              (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem.mk m law left center hleftState hcenterState
                (@HSub.hSub Real Real Real (@instHSub Real Real.instSub) t s) ⋯))
          (hrightDomain :
            @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.domain m law Result Information routine
              (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem.mk m law center right hcenterState hrightState
                (@HSub.hSub Real Real Real (@instHSub Real Real.instSub) t s) ⋯))
          (oldDensity newDensity leftPhysicalFlux rightPhysicalFlux : Real → Fin m → Real),
          @IntervalIntegrable (Fin m → Real)
              (@Pi.topologicalSpace (Fin m) (fun a => Real) fun i =>
                @UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace Real (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace Real Real.pseudoMetricSpace))
              (@NormedAddGroup.toENormedAddMonoid (Fin m → Real)
                (@Pi.normedAddGroup (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m) fun i =>
                  @NormedAddCommGroup.toNormedAddGroup Real Real.normedAddCommGroup))
              oldDensity (@MeasureTheory.MeasureSpace.volume Real Real.measureSpace) a b →
            @IntervalIntegrable (Fin m → Real)
                (@Pi.topologicalSpace (Fin m) (fun a => Real) fun i =>
                  @UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace Real (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace Real Real.pseudoMetricSpace))
                (@NormedAddGroup.toENormedAddMonoid (Fin m → Real)
                  (@Pi.normedAddGroup (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m) fun i =>
                    @NormedAddCommGroup.toNormedAddGroup Real Real.normedAddCommGroup))
                newDensity (@MeasureTheory.MeasureSpace.volume Real Real.measureSpace) a b →
              @IntervalIntegrable (Fin m → Real)
                  (@Pi.topologicalSpace (Fin m) (fun a => Real) fun i =>
                    @UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace Real
                      (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace Real Real.pseudoMetricSpace))
                  (@NormedAddGroup.toENormedAddMonoid (Fin m → Real)
                    (@Pi.normedAddGroup (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m) fun i =>
                      @NormedAddCommGroup.toNormedAddGroup Real Real.normedAddCommGroup))
                  leftPhysicalFlux (@MeasureTheory.MeasureSpace.volume Real Real.measureSpace) s t →
                @IntervalIntegrable (Fin m → Real)
                    (@Pi.topologicalSpace (Fin m) (fun a => Real) fun i =>
                      @UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace Real
                        (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace Real Real.pseudoMetricSpace))
                    (@NormedAddGroup.toENormedAddMonoid (Fin m → Real)
                      (@Pi.normedAddGroup (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m) fun i =>
                        @NormedAddCommGroup.toNormedAddGroup Real Real.normedAddCommGroup))
                    rightPhysicalFlux (@MeasureTheory.MeasureSpace.volume Real Real.measureSpace) s t →
                  @Eq (Fin m → Real)
                      (@HSub.hSub (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real)
                        (@instHSub (Fin m → Real) (@Pi.instSub (Fin m) (fun a => Real) fun i => Real.instSub))
                        (@intervalIntegral (Fin m → Real)
                          (@Pi.normedAddCommGroup (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m) fun i =>
                            Real.normedAddCommGroup)
                          (@Pi.normedSpace Real Real.normedField (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m)

                        (@HSub.hSub Real Real Real (@instHSub Real Real.instSub) t s) ⋯;

                    have rightFlux :=
                      @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.flux m law Result Information routine rightProblem

                    have next :=

                        (@HSub.hSub Real Real Real (@instHSub Real Real.instSub) b a) center
                        (@HSub.hSub (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real)

                    (fun x => field x t) a b (@MeasureTheory.MeasureSpace.volume Real Real.measureSpace))
                  (@intervalIntegral (Fin m → Real)

  @NumStability.oneDimensionalCellAverage (Fin m → Real)
    (@Pi.normedAddCommGroup (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m) fun i => Real.normedAddCommGroup)

@NumStability.IsRiemannData : {State : Type u_1} → (Real → State) → State → State → Prop
def NumStability.IsRiemannData.{u_1} : {State : Type u_1} → (Real → State) → State → State → Prop :=
fun {State} data leftState rightState =>
  And
    (∀ (x : Real),
      @LT.lt Real Real.instLT x (@OfNat.ofNat Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0 Real Real.instZero)) →
        @Eq State (data x) leftState)
    (∀ (x : Real),
      @LT.lt Real Real.instLT (@OfNat.ofNat Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0 Real Real.instZero)) x →
        @Eq State (data x) rightState)

@NumStability.riemannData : {State : Type u_1} → State → State → State → Real → State
def NumStability.riemannData.{u_1} : {State : Type u_1} → State → State → State → Real → State :=

  @ite State (@LT.lt Real Real.instLT x (@OfNat.ofNat Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0 Real Real.instZero)))
    (x.decidableLT (@OfNat.ofNat Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0 Real Real.instZero))) leftState
    (@ite State (@LT.lt Real Real.instLT (@OfNat.ofNat Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0 Real Real.instZero)) x)
      (Real.decidableLT (@OfNat.ofNat Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0 Real Real.instZero)) x) rightState valueAtOrigin)

  [inst : NormedAddCommGroup E] →
    [@NormedSpace Real E Real.normedField (@NormedAddCommGroup.toSeminormedAddCommGroup E inst)] →

    right =>

                        (@SeminormedAddCommGroup.toPseudoMetricSpace E
                          (@NormedAddCommGroup.toSeminormedAddCommGroup E inst))))
                    (@NormedAddCommGroup.toENormedAddCommMonoid E inst)))))))
        (@DistribSMul.toSMulZeroClass Real E
          (@AddMonoid.toAddZeroClass E
            (@ESeminormedAddMonoid.toAddMonoid E
              (@UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace E
                (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace E
                  (@SeminormedAddCommGroup.toPseudoMetricSpace E
                    (@NormedAddCommGroup.toSeminormedAddCommGroup E inst))))

          (@DistribMulAction.toDistribSMul Real E Real.instMonoid
            (@ESeminormedAddMonoid.toAddMonoid E
              (@UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace E
                (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace E

            (@Module.toDistribMulAction Real E Real.semiring
              (@ESeminormedAddCommMonoid.toAddCommMonoid E
                (@UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace E
                  (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace E
                    (@SeminormedAddCommGroup.toPseudoMetricSpace E

@NumStability.IsCellVolumeAverage : {Point : Type u_1} →
  {E : Type u_2} →
    [inst : MeasurableSpace Point] →
      [inst_1 : NormedAddCommGroup E] →
        [@NormedSpace Real E Real.normedField (@NormedAddCommGroup.toSeminormedAddCommGroup E inst_1)] →
          @MeasureTheory.Measure Point inst → Set Point → (Point → E) → E → Prop
def NumStability.IsCellVolumeAverage.{u_1, u_2} : {Point : Type u_1} →
  {E : Type u_2} →
    [inst : MeasurableSpace Point] →
      [inst_1 : NormedAddCommGroup E] →
        [@NormedSpace Real E Real.normedField (@NormedAddCommGroup.toSeminormedAddCommGroup E inst_1)] →
          @MeasureTheory.Measure Point inst → Set Point → (Point → E) → E → Prop :=
fun {Point} {E} [inst : MeasurableSpace Point] [inst_1 : NormedAddCommGroup E]
    [inst_2 : @NormedSpace Real E Real.normedField (@NormedAddCommGroup.toSeminormedAddCommGroup E inst_1)] μ region
    field average =>
  And
    (@Ne ENNReal
      (@DFunLike.coe (@MeasureTheory.Measure Point inst) (Set Point) (fun x => ENNReal)
        (@MeasureTheory.Measure.instFunLike Point inst) μ region)
      (@OfNat.ofNat ENNReal (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0 ENNReal instZeroENNReal)))
    (And
      (@Ne ENNReal
        (@DFunLike.coe (@MeasureTheory.Measure Point inst) (Set Point) (fun x => ENNReal)
          (@MeasureTheory.Measure.instFunLike Point inst) μ region)
        (@Top.top ENNReal instTopENNReal))
      (And
        (@MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn Point E inst
          (@UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace E
            (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace E
              (@SeminormedAddCommGroup.toPseudoMetricSpace E (@NormedAddCommGroup.toSeminormedAddCommGroup E inst_1))))
          (@SeminormedAddGroup.toContinuousENorm E
            (@SeminormedAddCommGroup.toSeminormedAddGroup E (@NormedAddCommGroup.toSeminormedAddCommGroup E inst_1)))
          field region μ)
        (@Eq E average (@NumStability.cellVolumeAverage Point E inst inst_1 inst_2 μ region field))))

    [@Module Real E Real.semiring (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid E inst)] → Real → Real → E → E → E
def NumStability.finiteVolumeCellAverageUpdate.{u_1} : {E : Type u_1} →
  [inst : AddCommGroup E] →
    [@Module Real E Real.semiring (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid E inst)] → Real → Real → E → E → E :=
fun {E} [inst : AddCommGroup E] [inst_1 : @Module Real E Real.semiring (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid E inst)] timeStep
    cellVolume oldAverage netOutwardFlux =>
  @HSub.hSub E E E (@instHSub E (@SubNegMonoid.toSub E (@AddGroup.toSubNegMonoid E (@AddCommGroup.toAddGroup E inst))))
    oldAverage
    (@HSMul.hSMul Real E E
      (@instHSMul Real E
        (@SMulZeroClass.toSMul Real E
          (@AddZero.toZero E
            (@AddZeroClass.toAddZero E
              (@AddMonoid.toAddZeroClass E
                (@SubNegMonoid.toAddMonoid E (@AddGroup.toSubNegMonoid E (@AddCommGroup.toAddGroup E inst))))))
          (@DistribSMul.toSMulZeroClass Real E
            (@AddMonoid.toAddZeroClass E
              (@SubNegMonoid.toAddMonoid E (@AddGroup.toSubNegMonoid E (@AddCommGroup.toAddGroup E inst))))
            (@DistribMulAction.toDistribSMul Real E Real.instMonoid
              (@SubNegMonoid.toAddMonoid E (@AddGroup.toSubNegMonoid E (@AddCommGroup.toAddGroup E inst)))
              (@Module.toDistribMulAction Real E Real.semiring (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid E inst) inst_1)))))
      (@HDiv.hDiv Real Real Real (@instHDiv Real (@DivInvMonoid.toDiv Real Real.instDivInvMonoid)) timeStep cellVolume)
      netOutwardFlux)
